# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 026 BLACK FRIDAY EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# Event-owned lifecycle for the actorless global sale. Selection reserves the
# event, while the existing bounded global pulse activates it on the first
# eligible Friday. Activation snapshots one payment ratio, records one
# fire-once history row, refreshes human status markers, and expires on the
# next daily tick. No recurring country-wide scan is introduced here.
# ============================================================================

black_friday_update_calendar_flag = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_calendar_remainder = global.num_days }
	modulo_temp_variable = {
		black_friday_calendar_remainder = constant:black_friday_event.weekday_cycle
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				black_friday_calendar_remainder = constant:black_friday_event.friday_remainder
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = black_friday_friday
	}
	else = {
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_friday
	}
}

black_friday_initialize_runtime = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = black_friday_runtime_initialized } }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_state = constant:black_friday_event.state_unavailable }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_ratio = constant:black_friday_event.baseline_payment_ratio }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_percent = constant:black_friday_event.baseline_discount_percent }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_reservation_date = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_activation_date = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_expiration_date = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_sale_sequence = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_achievement_family_count = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_reserved
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_active
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_natural_reservation
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_natural_activation
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_force_disqualified
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_manual_disqualified
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_evolution_i_recorded
		set_global_flag = black_friday_runtime_initialized
	}
	black_friday_update_calendar_flag = yes
	black_friday_price_table_initialize = yes
}

black_friday_prepare_achievement_progress = {
	clear_array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids
	set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_count = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
	set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_active_sale_sequence = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
	set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
	set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
	clr_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_has_institutional_family
	clr_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_has_material_family
	clr_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_native_non_political_payment
	clr_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_engine_non_political_payment
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Engine-priced purchases for the achievement
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Law changes, advisor and staff hires, experience spending, and command power
# spending are priced by the engine through the sale ideas, so no owner effect
# sees those payments. Activation snapshots each human country that holds a
# valid natural sale, inside the existing activation country loop. Expiry runs
# one bounded human-country loop while the sale is still active, compares the
# snapshot with the current state, and credits the matching family through the
# shared committed-family path. A law, slot, or resource change made by a focus
# or event on the sale day also counts, because the engine has no law-change
# or hire hook. Spending smaller than the same day's experience or command
# power gain is not detected.

# COUNTRY scope. Outputs: temps black_friday_engine_economy_law,
# black_friday_engine_trade_law, and black_friday_engine_mobilization_law, each
# a constant:black_friday_engine_law id, or unknown for a law outside the table.
black_friday_engine_purchase_read_laws = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.unknown }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = undisturbed_isolation } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.undisturbed_isolation } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = isolation } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.isolation } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = civilian_economy } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.civilian_economy } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = low_economic_mobilisation } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.low_economic_mobilisation } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = partial_economic_mobilisation } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.partial_economic_mobilisation } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = war_economy } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.war_economy } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = tot_economic_mobilisation } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.tot_economic_mobilisation } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = totaler_krieg_economy } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.totaler_krieg_economy } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = national_defense_state } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.national_defense_state } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = new_economic_policy } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.new_economic_policy } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = new_economic_policy_2 } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.new_economic_policy_2 } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = capital_investment_model } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.capital_investment_model } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = JAP_war_communism_idea } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.jap_war_communism_idea } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = SPR_collectivized_society } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.spr_collectivized_society } }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.unknown }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = uncontrolled_exports } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.uncontrolled_exports } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = free_trade } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.free_trade } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = export_focus } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.export_focus } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = limited_exports } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.limited_exports } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = autarkic_economy } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.autarkic_economy } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = autarkic_economy_improved } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.autarkic_economy_improved } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = closed_economy } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.closed_economy } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = special_economic_zones } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.special_economic_zones } }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.unknown }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = disarmed_nation } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.disarmed_nation } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = volunteer_only } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.volunteer_only } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = limited_conscription } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.limited_conscription } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = extensive_conscription } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.extensive_conscription } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = service_by_requirement } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.service_by_requirement } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = all_adults_serve } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.all_adults_serve } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.scraping_the_barrel } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = ETH_chitet_law } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.eth_chitet_law } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = ETH_chitet_law_peace_time } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.eth_chitet_law_peace_time } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = SWI_citizen_militia_1 } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.swi_citizen_militia_1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_idea = SWI_citizen_militia_2 } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = constant:black_friday_engine_law.swi_citizen_militia_2 } }
}

# COUNTRY scope. Outputs: temps black_friday_engine_advisor_count (filled
# political advisor slots) and black_friday_engine_staff_count (filled theorist,
# chief, and high command slots), each counted up to
# constant:black_friday_event.engine_slot_count_limit.
black_friday_engine_purchase_count_slots = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_advisor_count = constant:black_friday_event.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { black_friday_engine_advisor_count < constant:black_friday_event.engine_slot_count_limit }
			meta_trigger = {
				text = { amount_taken_ideas = { amount > [COUNT] slots = { political_advisor } } }
				COUNT = "[?black_friday_engine_advisor_count|.0]"
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_advisor_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_staff_count = constant:black_friday_event.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { black_friday_engine_staff_count < constant:black_friday_event.engine_slot_count_limit }
			meta_trigger = {
				text = { amount_taken_ideas = { amount > [COUNT] slots = { theorist army_chief navy_chief air_chief high_command } } }
				COUNT = "[?black_friday_engine_staff_count|.0]"
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_staff_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope, human country at sale activation. Records the engine-priced
# purchase baseline for this sale when the country holds a valid natural sale.
black_friday_engine_purchase_snapshot = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_achievement_is_valid_sale = yes }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_sale_sequence = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
		black_friday_engine_purchase_read_laws = yes
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_economy_law = black_friday_engine_economy_law }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_trade_law = black_friday_engine_trade_law }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_mobilization_law = black_friday_engine_mobilization_law }
		black_friday_engine_purchase_count_slots = yes
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_advisor_count = black_friday_engine_advisor_count }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_staff_count = black_friday_engine_staff_count }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_army_experience = army_experience }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_navy_experience = navy_experience }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_air_experience = air_experience }
		set_variable = { black_friday_engine_snapshot_command_power = command_power }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope, human country at sale expiry, before the sale source clears.
# Credits laws for any changed law category, personnel for more political
# advisors, officer corps for more theorist, chief, or high command holders, and
# the matching experience or command power family for a net decrease. The
# experience and command power credits are also the payment in a resource other
# than political power. Every disqualifier of the achievement still applies
# through black_friday_engine_purchase_can_credit. The snapshot is cleared.
black_friday_engine_purchase_credit = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_engine_purchase_can_credit = yes }
		black_friday_engine_purchase_read_laws = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_economy_law = black_friday_engine_snapshot_economy_law } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_trade_law = black_friday_engine_snapshot_trade_law } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_mobilization_law = black_friday_engine_snapshot_mobilization_law } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.laws }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
		black_friday_engine_purchase_count_slots = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_advisor_count > black_friday_engine_snapshot_advisor_count } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.personnel }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_staff_count > black_friday_engine_snapshot_staff_count } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.officer_corps }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount = army_experience }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount < black_friday_engine_snapshot_army_experience } }
			set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_engine_non_political_payment
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.army_experience }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount = navy_experience }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount < black_friday_engine_snapshot_navy_experience } }
			set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_engine_non_political_payment
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.navy_experience }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount = air_experience }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount < black_friday_engine_snapshot_air_experience } }
			set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_engine_non_political_payment
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.air_experience }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount = command_power }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_engine_current_amount < black_friday_engine_snapshot_command_power } }
			set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_engine_non_political_payment
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.command_power }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
	}
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_sale_sequence
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_economy_law
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_trade_law
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_mobilization_law
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_advisor_count
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_staff_count
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_army_experience
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_navy_experience
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_air_experience
	clear_variable = black_friday_engine_snapshot_command_power
}

# Ordinary receipt proof: typed owner family and explicit primary-cost basis.
# Scope: payer. Defaults: invalid proof for unknown/mismatched role metadata.
# Inputs are caller claims plus black_friday_receipt_required_state (1 pending,
# 4 committed). Outputs must be defined by caller before this helper invocation.
# Side effects: lazy normal metadata-array preparation only; no payment or credit.
black_friday_validate_ordinary_receipt_proof = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	universal_cost_prepare_achievement_receipt_metadata = yes
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_lookup_id = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_found_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_is_indexed = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_pointer = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	universal_cost_find_transaction_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_is_indexed = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_ids^universal_cost_transaction_found_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_states^universal_cost_transaction_found_index = black_friday_receipt_required_state }
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_total_ordinary_costs^universal_cost_transaction_found_index > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_total_paid_costs^universal_cost_transaction_found_index > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_total_refunded_costs^universal_cost_transaction_found_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_ids^universal_cost_transaction_found_index = black_friday_purchase_family_id }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.command_power }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.trains }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.convoy }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.fuel }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.manpower }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.laws }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.personnel }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.officer_corps }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.intelligence }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.factory_commitment }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.consumer_goods }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.special_project }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.army_experience }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.navy_experience }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.air_experience }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.stability }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.war_support }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.advisor }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.idea }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.operation }
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment_design }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { black_friday_receipt_required_state = constant:universal_cost_framework.transaction_state_recorded }
				check_variable = { black_friday_receipt_required_state = constant:universal_cost_framework.transaction_state_settled }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_chain_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_validate_component_chain = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_chain_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_basis = universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_bases^universal_cost_transaction_found_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_primary = universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_ids^universal_cost_transaction_found_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_bad = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_pointer = universal_cost_transaction_component_head_plus_one^universal_cost_transaction_found_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_walked = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_all_ordinary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_ordinary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_pointer > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_proof_walked value = universal_cost_transaction_component_counts^universal_cost_transaction_found_index compare = less_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_cursor = black_friday_proof_pointer }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { AND = {
							check_variable = { universal_cost_component_source_ids^black_friday_proof_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
							check_variable = { universal_cost_component_refunded_states^black_friday_proof_cursor = constant:universal_cost_component_state.not_refunded }
							check_variable = { universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor = universal_cost_component_quoted_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor }
							check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_ordinary_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { universal_cost_component_achievement_family_ids^black_friday_proof_cursor = black_friday_purchase_family_id }
							OR = {
								check_variable = { universal_cost_component_achievement_primary_members^black_friday_proof_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
								check_variable = { universal_cost_component_achievement_primary_members^black_friday_proof_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
							}
						} }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_bad = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_all_ordinary = universal_cost_component_ordinary_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_all_paid = universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
						check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_resource_kinds^black_friday_proof_cursor value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power compare = not_equals }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_component_achievement_primary_members^black_friday_proof_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								AND = {
									check_variable = { black_friday_proof_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
									check_variable = { universal_cost_component_ids^black_friday_proof_cursor = black_friday_proof_primary }
								}
								AND = {
									check_variable = { black_friday_proof_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_group }
									check_variable = { black_friday_proof_primary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_ordinary = universal_cost_component_ordinary_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor }
						add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_paid = universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^black_friday_proof_cursor }
						add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
					else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_bad = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_pointer = universal_cost_component_next_plus_one^black_friday_proof_cursor }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_proof_walked = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_bad = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_pointer = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_walked = universal_cost_transaction_component_counts^universal_cost_transaction_found_index }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_all_ordinary = universal_cost_transaction_total_ordinary_costs^universal_cost_transaction_found_index }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_all_paid = universal_cost_transaction_total_paid_costs^universal_cost_transaction_found_index }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_ordinary > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_paid > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_ordinary = black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost }
					check_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_paid = black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { black_friday_proof_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
							check_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { black_friday_proof_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_group }
							check_variable = { black_friday_proof_selected_count > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = black_friday_proof_non_political }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Scope: payer. Inputs: public family/ordinary/paid claim and pending marker.
# Outputs: caller-owned proof-valid/nonPP temporaries. Defaults: invalid proof.
# Side effects: metadata schema preparation only, never family credit.
black_friday_scan_receipt_non_political_payment = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_receipt_required_state = constant:universal_cost_framework.transaction_state_settled }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_receipt_required_state = constant:universal_cost_framework.transaction_state_recorded }
	}
	black_friday_validate_ordinary_receipt_proof = yes
}

# A timed PP-only owner action may credit its PP family only after its
# exact Black Friday component is settled. Ordinary achievement calls keep the
# existing non-political payment scan above.
# Scope: payer. Inputs: exact PP-primary claim. Outputs: caller-owned proof and
# payment evidence. Defaults: invalid. Side effects: schema preparation only.
# Requires settled/unrefunded PP-only payment, including accepted delayed owners.
black_friday_scan_receipt_political_power_only = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_receipt_required_state = constant:universal_cost_framework.transaction_state_settled }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power } }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_found_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
		black_friday_validate_ordinary_receipt_proof = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					check_variable = { var = universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_ids^universal_cost_transaction_found_index value = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power compare = not_equals }
					check_variable = { var = universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_bases^universal_cost_transaction_found_index value = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component compare = not_equals }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		}
	}
}

# Shared primary-family credit accepts either a committed universal receipt or
# a proven native equipment reservation. The caller supplies the family ID;
# non-political payment is tracked from actual committed receipts and raids.
black_friday_credit_achievement_family = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids
					value = black_friday_purchase_family_id
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids
			value = black_friday_purchase_family_id
		}
		add_to_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.laws
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.personnel
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.officer_corps
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.intelligence
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_has_institutional_family
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.convoys
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.trains
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.fuel
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.factory_commitment
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.consumer_goods
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.special_project
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_purchase_family_id
						value = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_has_material_family
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_achievement_family_count
				value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_target_family_count
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			OR = {
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.laws }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.personnel }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.officer_corps }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.intelligence }
			}
			OR = {
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.convoys }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.trains }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.fuel }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.factory_commitment }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.consumer_goods }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.special_project }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project }
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_native_non_political_payment
				has_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_engine_non_political_payment
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count value = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_awarded
	}
}

# Native sale raids have no universal-cost receipt callback. Their unique
# discounted raid definition reserves real payload equipment when prepared;
# the owner calls this only after a successful accepted outcome.
black_friday_credit_native_japan_cbrn_raid_family = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_achievement_can_credit_native_japan_raid = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_native_non_political_payment
		black_friday_credit_achievement_family = yes
	}
}

# Input: black_friday_purchase_family_id. Count only committed receipts from
# this sale; native raid family proof remains the separate country flag.
black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_family_generation_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = not_equals } }
			set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_generation_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
			set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_committed_count_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		}
		add_to_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_committed_count_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		black_friday_credit_achievement_family = yes
	}
}

black_friday_remove_committed_achievement_family = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_family_generation_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_family_committed_count_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id value = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date compare = greater_than }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_committed_count_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_family_committed_count_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id value = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date compare = equals }
				is_in_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = black_friday_purchase_family_id }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_awarded }
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_native_non_political_payment
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_purchase_family_id value = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment compare = equals }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_from_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_family_ids value = black_friday_purchase_family_id }
			subtract_from_variable = { black_friday_achievement_family_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: black_friday_purchase_transaction_id, black_friday_purchase_family_id,
# black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost, black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost,
# and optional positive black_friday_purchase_is_refundable. Owners call this
# after the shared receipt was recorded and after the real debit succeeded.
# Lazy claim-column extension preserves old progress and pending row states.
# Legacy rows retain unknown (zero) amounts until an exact owner proof exists.
# Scope: payer. Inputs: achievement row count. Outputs: normal claim columns.
# Defaults: unknown zero suffixes. Side effects: idempotent resize only; existing
# credited families, counters, pending/refund states and pointers are untouched.
black_friday_prepare_achievement_claim_metadata = {
	resize_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ordinary_costs size = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^num }
	resize_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_paid_costs size = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^num }
}

black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = {
	black_friday_prepare_achievement_claim_metadata = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_achievement_pointer = black_friday_achievement_row_plus_one_@var:black_friday_purchase_transaction_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				black_friday_achievement_is_valid_sale = yes
				AND = {
					has_variable = black_friday_purchase_allow_post_expiry_confirmation
					check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_post_expiry_confirmation > 0 }
					has_variable = black_friday_purchase_allow_political_power_only
					check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_political_power_only > 0 }
					black_friday_achievement_possible_campaign_is_valid = yes
					has_global_flag = black_friday_natural_activation
					NOT = { has_global_flag = black_friday_force_disqualified }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = black_friday_manual_disqualified }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = black_friday_achievement_awarded }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_purchase_family_id value = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_purchase_sale_sequence value = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_active_sale_sequence value = black_friday_purchase_sale_sequence compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence value = black_friday_purchase_sale_sequence compare = equals }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id > 0 }
			check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id > 0 }
			check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost > 0 }
			check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_purchase_achievement_pointer value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = universal_cost_transaction_row_plus_one_@var:black_friday_purchase_transaction_id value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = black_friday_purchase_allow_political_power_only
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_political_power_only > 0 }
			}
			black_friday_scan_receipt_political_power_only = yes
		}
		else = { black_friday_scan_receipt_non_political_payment = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_active_sale_sequence value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = not_equals }
			}
			black_friday_prepare_achievement_progress = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids
				value = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_family_ids
				value = black_friday_purchase_family_id
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_sale_sequences
				value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_non_political_paid
				value = black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component
			}
			add_to_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ordinary_costs value = black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost }
			add_to_array = { array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_paid_costs value = black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = black_friday_purchase_is_refundable
					check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable > 0 }
				}
				add_to_array = {
					array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_states
					value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_pending
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = {
					array = black_friday_achievement_transaction_states
					value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_committed
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
					add_to_variable = { black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
				}
				black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_achievement_pointer = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^num }
			set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_row_plus_one_@var:black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_purchase_achievement_pointer }
		}
	}
}

black_friday_find_achievement_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_row_pointer = black_friday_achievement_row_plus_one_@var:black_friday_purchase_transaction_id }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_row_pointer value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_candidate_index = black_friday_achievement_row_pointer }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_candidate_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_candidate_index value = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^black_friday_achievement_candidate_index value = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = black_friday_achievement_candidate_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_rejected } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^num value = constant:universal_cost_framework.legacy_scan_max_rows compare = less_than_or_equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_cursor = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_cursor value = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index compare = equals }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ids^black_friday_achievement_transaction_cursor value = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = black_friday_achievement_transaction_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_cursor = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		}
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_rejected } }
}

black_friday_confirm_achievement_transaction = {
	black_friday_prepare_achievement_claim_metadata = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			black_friday_achievement_is_valid_sale = yes
			check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_row_pointer = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_find_achievement_transaction = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success }
				check_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_index > constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_pending }
				check_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_sale_sequences^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
				check_variable = { black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = black_friday_achievement_transaction_family_ids^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = black_friday_achievement_transaction_ordinary_costs^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_achievement_transaction_paid_costs^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_receipt_required_state = constant:universal_cost_framework.transaction_state_settled }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_validate_ordinary_receipt_proof = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_receipt_proof_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
				set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_committed }
				set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_non_political_paid^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_has_non_political_paid_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
					add_to_variable = { black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
				}
				black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

black_friday_retract_achievement_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id > 0 } }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_row_pointer = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_find_achievement_transaction = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_lookup_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_row_pointer value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index compare = greater_than }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_refunded compare = equals } }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_retraction_allowed = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = black_friday_achievement_transaction_family_ids^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_committed compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_sale_sequences^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = not_equals }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_family_generation_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = not_equals }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_family_committed_count_@var:black_friday_purchase_family_id value = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date compare = less_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_achievement_retraction_allowed = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_retraction_allowed value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_committed compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_non_political_paid^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count value = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_sale_sequences^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals }
					}
					subtract_from_variable = { black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_committed compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_achievement_transaction_sale_sequences^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index value = global.black_friday_sale_sequence compare = equals }
					}
					black_friday_remove_committed_achievement_family = yes
				}
				set_variable = { black_friday_achievement_transaction_states^black_friday_achievement_transaction_index = constant:black_friday_event.achievement_transaction_refunded }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Every country that received the sale is cleared, including one that ceased
# to exist during the sale day, so a later release cannot inherit the discount.
black_friday_clear_active_status = {
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = black_friday_native_sale_stored
				has_idea = black_friday_sale
				has_idea = black_friday_sale_evolution_i
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = black_friday_sale
		remove_ideas = black_friday_sale_evolution_i
		black_friday_native_sale_remove = yes
	}
}

# Apply the fire-once pacing transaction without recalculating the event timer.
# The selection transaction already resolved that timer when the reservation
# was created. A delayed Friday activation must therefore update the pacing
# counters and major-event gain in place while allowing that timer to continue.
black_friday_apply_activation_pacing = {
	add_to_variable = { global.total_events_fired = constant:black_friday_event.one }
	add_to_variable = { global.minor_events_since_major = constant:black_friday_event.one }
	add_to_variable = { timer_day_decrement = constant:event_system_timer_defaults.minor_event_decrement_step }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = timer_day_decrement
		max = timer_min_decrement_cap
	}
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_pacing_timer_check = global.minor_events_since_major }
	modulo_temp_variable = {
		black_friday_pacing_timer_check = constant:event_system_timer_defaults.minor_event_timer_increment
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_pacing_timer_check
				value = constant:black_friday_event.zero
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = timer_max_cap_reduction
				value = timer_max_decrement_cap
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { timer_max_cap_reduction = constant:event_system_timer_defaults.minor_event_compression_step }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = timer_max_cap_reduction
				value = timer_max_decrement_cap
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { timer_max_cap_reduction = timer_max_decrement_cap }
	}
	update_repeatable_event_weights = yes
	apply_dynamic_major_weight_gain_after_minor = yes
	update_unfired_events_counts = yes
}

black_friday_refresh_human_status = {
	black_friday_refresh_active_cost_modifier = yes
}

# Allocate one payer-scoped transaction id for an owner adapter.
black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = black_friday_transaction_sequence } }
		set_variable = { black_friday_transaction_sequence = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
	}
	add_to_variable = { black_friday_transaction_sequence = constant:black_friday_event.one }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = black_friday_transaction_sequence }
	set_variable = { universal_cost_allocated_transaction_id_@var:black_friday_owner_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
}

# The owner-family bridge below accepts one logical Fury purchase as four
# aligned temporary arrays. Each component is quoted, preflighted, paid, and
# receipted before the action's normal effect is allowed to continue. The
# primary component supplies the single achievement family for the logical
# action; secondary components never create a second achievement family.
black_friday_fury_begin_component_list = {
	clear_temp_array = black_friday_fury_component_ids
	clear_temp_array = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds
	clear_temp_array = black_friday_fury_component_family_masks
	clear_temp_array = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_costs
}

# Inputs: black_friday_fury_next_component_id,
# black_friday_fury_next_resource_kind, black_friday_fury_next_family_mask,
# and black_friday_fury_next_ordinary_cost.
black_friday_fury_append_component = {
	add_to_temp_array = {
		array = black_friday_fury_component_ids
		value = black_friday_fury_next_component_id
	}
	add_to_temp_array = {
		array = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds
		value = black_friday_fury_next_resource_kind
	}
	add_to_temp_array = {
		array = black_friday_fury_component_family_masks
		value = black_friday_fury_next_family_mask
	}
	add_to_temp_array = {
		array = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_costs
		value = black_friday_fury_next_ordinary_cost
	}
}

# Inputs are the same as black_friday_fury_append_component, except that
# black_friday_fury_next_spend is the owner's negative payment constant. The
# shared ledger stores positive ordinary costs, so the sign is inverted before
# the component is appended.
black_friday_fury_append_spend_component = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_next_ordinary_cost = black_friday_fury_next_spend }
	multiply_temp_variable = {
		black_friday_fury_next_ordinary_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.negative_one
	}
	black_friday_fury_append_component = yes
}

# Inputs are the aligned arrays prepared by black_friday_fury_append_component,
# black_friday_fury_primary_component_id, and
# black_friday_fury_primary_family_id. Output:
# black_friday_fury_payment_success. All resources are native framework kinds,
# so the generic refund path can return the actual paid amounts if a later
# component or receipt operation fails.
black_friday_pay_fury_bundle = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	set_temp_variable = {
		black_friday_fury_component_count = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_costs^num
	}
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_primary_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	clear_temp_array = black_friday_fury_component_final_costs
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { universal_cost_source_is_active = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_costs
		value = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_cost
		index = black_friday_fury_component_index
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_quote_family_mask = black_friday_fury_component_family_masks^black_friday_fury_component_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds^black_friday_fury_component_index
					value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.stability
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_fixed_point_quantum.stability }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds^black_friday_fury_component_index
					value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.war_support
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_fixed_point_quantum.war_support }
		}
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		add_to_temp_array = {
			array = black_friday_fury_component_final_costs
			value = universal_cost_quote_final_cost
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_fury_component_ids^black_friday_fury_component_index
					value = black_friday_fury_primary_component_id
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_primary_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_primary_ordinary = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_cost }
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds^black_friday_fury_component_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = universal_cost_quote_affordable
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = not_equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_fury_all_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_break = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
					value = black_friday_fury_component_count
					compare = less_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_fury_payment_break
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds^black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_fury_component_final_costs^black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			universal_cost_pay_component = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = universal_cost_payment_result
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_fury_component_ids^black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds^black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
				}
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = {
					universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_costs^black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_fury_component_final_costs^black_friday_fury_payment_cursor
				}
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = universal_cost_transaction_component_id
						value = black_friday_fury_primary_component_id
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = black_friday_fury_primary_component_id }
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = black_friday_fury_primary_family_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = black_friday_fury_primary_component_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_fury_primary_component_id } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = not_equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_break = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_break = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			black_friday_fury_payment_cursor = constant:universal_cost_framework.one
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_fury_all_affordable
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = black_friday_fury_primary_family_id }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = black_friday_fury_primary_ordinary }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_fury_primary_paid }
			black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
		}
	}
}

# Active-sale helper for a one-component owner action. Inputs are the four
# `black_friday_fury_single_*` fields and the selected primary achievement
# family. The ordinary path remains in each owner's original payment helper.
black_friday_fury_pay_single_active = {
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	resize_temp_array = { array = black_friday_fury_component_ids size = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	resize_temp_array = { array = black_friday_fury_component_resource_kinds size = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	resize_temp_array = { array = black_friday_fury_component_family_masks size = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	resize_temp_array = { array = black_friday_fury_component_ordinary_costs size = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	black_friday_fury_begin_component_list = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_next_component_id = black_friday_fury_single_component_id }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_next_resource_kind = black_friday_fury_single_resource_kind }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_next_family_mask = black_friday_fury_single_family_mask }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_next_ordinary_cost = black_friday_fury_single_ordinary_cost }
	black_friday_fury_append_component = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_primary_component_id = black_friday_fury_single_component_id }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_primary_family_id = black_friday_fury_single_primary_family_id }
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	black_friday_pay_fury_bundle = yes
}

# Active-sale helper for a multi-component owner action. Callers prepare the
# aligned component arrays and provide black_friday_fury_primary_component_id
# and black_friday_fury_primary_family_id before calling it.
black_friday_fury_pay_prepared_bundle_active = {
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	black_friday_pay_fury_bundle = yes
}

# Event 007 owner adapters. Each effect keeps the original full-price debit
# when no sale is active and exposes one success result for its caller. The
# active branches quote every native component through the shared transaction
# adapter; penalties, rewards, targets, and state changes remain in the owner
# effects outside these payment blocks.
black_friday_fury_pay_target_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_primary_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_ordinary_cost = constant:fury_decision_cost.target_cp }
		black_friday_fury_pay_single_active = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_command_power = constant:fury_decision_cost.target_cp_spend
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
}
