}

black_friday_fury_pay_anti_recognition_denial_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_primary_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_single_ordinary_cost = constant:fury_decision_cost.anti_fury_recognition_cp }
		black_friday_fury_pay_single_active = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_command_power = constant:fury_decision_cost.anti_fury_recognition_cp_spend
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_fury_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
}

# Country-scope adapter for the Communist national counter-agitation decision.
# The operation's ordinary current costs are quoted, paid, receipted, and then
# settled as one logical action with political power as its primary family.
black_friday_pay_communism_national_counter_agitation = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	prepare_communism_national_action_costs = yes
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = communism_counter_agitation_command_power_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.command_power }
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_command_power_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = communism_counter_agitation_political_power_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
	universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }

	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_command_power_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
	universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_owner_command_power_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_owner_political_power_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_command_power_payment = universal_cost_payment_result }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_payment = universal_cost_payment_result }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_owner_command_power_payment
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_owner_political_power_payment
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_command_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = communism_counter_agitation_command_power_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_command_power_record = universal_cost_record_transaction_result }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = communism_counter_agitation_political_power_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_record = universal_cost_record_transaction_result }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_owner_command_power_record
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_owner_political_power_record
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = communism_counter_agitation_political_power_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
				black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_owner_manpower_paid }
	universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_manpower_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.infantry_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_paid }
	universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
	universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_owner_command_power_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_owner_manpower_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_owner_political_power_affordable
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_command_power_payment = universal_cost_payment_result }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_owner_manpower_paid }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_manpower_payment = universal_cost_payment_result }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.infantry_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_paid }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_payment = universal_cost_payment_result }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_payment = universal_cost_payment_result }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_owner_command_power_payment
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_owner_manpower_payment
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_payment
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = black_friday_owner_political_power_payment
					value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_command_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = communism_emergency_command_power_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_command_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_command_power_record = universal_cost_record_transaction_result }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_manpower }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.manpower }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = communism_emergency_manpower_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_owner_manpower_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_manpower_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_manpower_record = universal_cost_record_transaction_result }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.infantry_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = communism_emergency_equipment_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_record = universal_cost_record_transaction_result }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = communism_emergency_political_power_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_political_power_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_infantry_equipment } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_political_power_record = universal_cost_record_transaction_result }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_owner_command_power_record
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_owner_manpower_record
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_record
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = black_friday_owner_political_power_record
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = communism_emergency_equipment_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_owner_infantry_equipment_paid }
				black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. Shows the sale idea and applies the native sale modifier while
# a sale is active, and removes both otherwise. The native contributions are
# recomputed from the country's current ordinary factors on every call
# (common/scripted_effects/026_black_friday_native_sale_effects.txt).
black_friday_refresh_active_cost_modifier = {
	remove_ideas = black_friday_sale
	remove_ideas = black_friday_sale_evolution_i
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.black_friday_discount_ratio
					value = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_payment_ratio
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			add_ideas = black_friday_sale_evolution_i
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = black_friday_sale
		}
		black_friday_native_sale_apply = yes
	}
	else = {
		black_friday_native_sale_remove = yes
	}
}

black_friday_cancel_reservation = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_reserved = yes }
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_reserved
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_natural_reservation
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_state = constant:black_friday_event.state_canceled }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_reservation_date = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
		log = "CHAOS REDUX BLACK FRIDAY: Global reservation canceled without a fire"
	}
}

black_friday_on_event_disabled = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_disabled = yes
			black_friday_is_reserved = yes
		}
		black_friday_cancel_reservation = yes
	}
}

black_friday_expire_sale = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		every_country = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			black_friday_engine_purchase_credit = yes
		}
		universal_cost_source_clear_black_friday = yes
		black_friday_price_table_restore = yes
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_active
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_state = constant:black_friday_event.state_expired }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_expiration_date = global.num_days }
		black_friday_clear_active_status = yes
		log = "CHAOS REDUX BLACK FRIDAY: Sale expired on the daily pulse"
	}
}

black_friday_terminal_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_reserved = yes }
		black_friday_cancel_reservation = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		black_friday_expire_sale = yes
	}
}

black_friday_record_evolution_i = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = black_friday_evolution_i_recorded }
			check_variable = {
				global.black_friday_discount_ratio = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_payment_ratio
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:black_friday_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_stage_i }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_tier_i }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 0 }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
		set_global_flag = black_friday_evolution_i_recorded
	}
}

black_friday_record_activation_history = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = black_friday_history_recorded } }
		set_variable = { global.last_fired_event_id = constant:black_friday_event.id }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { global.fired_events = constant:black_friday_event.id } } }
			add_to_array = { global.fired_events = constant:black_friday_event.id }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { event_id = constant:black_friday_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { new_weight = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		set_event_weight = yes
		black_friday_apply_activation_pacing = yes
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_event_id = constant:black_friday_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_event_type = constant:event_system_event_type.fire_once }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_payload = global.black_friday_discount_ratio }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_actor = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_has_actor = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_secondary_actor = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_has_secondary_actor = 0 }
		record_events_log_system_history_entry = yes
		refresh_events_log_system_history_views = yes
		set_global_flag = black_friday_history_recorded
		update_last_fired_event_display = yes
		log = "CHAOS REDUX BLACK FRIDAY: Activation recorded as one actorless fire-once event"
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Biological medical-capacity owner adapter
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope. This is the upfront payment for the delayed medical-capacity
# investment. The decision's native PP field is suppressed so this adapter can
# quote and debit PP, support equipment, and motorized equipment atomically.
black_friday_record_bio_medical_component = {
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_bio_medical_current_component_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = black_friday_bio_medical_current_resource_kind }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_bio_medical_current_ordinary_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_bio_medical_current_paid }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_bio_medical_current_paid }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_bio_medical_current_component_id
				value = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
	}
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
	# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment } }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
	universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = not_equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_bio_medical_current_resource_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_bio_medical_current_paid }
		universal_cost_credit_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	}
}

black_friday_pay_bio_expand_medical_capacity = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
		if = {
			limit = {
				universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
				bio_countermeasure_can_expand_medical_capacity_prerequisites = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_pp }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_pp_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_pp_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }

			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_support }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
			universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_support_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment }
			universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_support_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }

			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_trucks }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
			universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_truck_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment }
			universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_truck_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_bio_medical_pp_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_bio_medical_support_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_bio_medical_truck_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
				}
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }

				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_bio_medical_pp_paid }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_pp }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_paid = black_friday_bio_medical_pp_paid }
					black_friday_record_bio_medical_component = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}

				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_bio_medical_support_paid }
					universal_cost_pay_component = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_support }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_paid = black_friday_bio_medical_support_paid }
						black_friday_record_bio_medical_component = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_bio_medical_truck_paid }
					universal_cost_pay_component = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_motorized_equipment }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_trucks }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_current_paid = black_friday_bio_medical_truck_paid }
						black_friday_record_bio_medical_component = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_bio_medical_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_support }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_bio_medical_support_paid }
				black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
				set_country_flag = black_friday_bio_medical_investment_paid
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_pp_debit = constant:bio_countermeasure_cost.expand_medical_pp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { black_friday_bio_medical_pp_debit = constant:universal_cost_framework.negative_one }
		add_political_power = black_friday_bio_medical_pp_debit
		bio_countermeasure_pay_expand_medical_capacity = yes
		set_country_flag = black_friday_bio_medical_investment_paid
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CBRN diplomacy instrument/support owner adapters
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Country scope. One preflighted concrete component of an instrument/support purchase.
# A failed receipt or a mismatched physical debit is credited immediately; prior
# recorded components are refunded by the calling transaction.
black_friday_cbrn_bundle_pay_record_component = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_after = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_after = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_after = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_after = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_after = num_equipment@support_equipment_1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_before }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_after }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual value = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_ordinary }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_id }
				}
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_family_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_group }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { OR = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 } } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual }
					universal_cost_credit_component = yes
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_actual }
					universal_cost_credit_component = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
	}
}

# Country scope. Rechecks the sale, aggregate quote, and every concrete
# stockpile before allocating one payer-scoped receipt. All model draws use the
# pre-debit balances, oldest first. Ordinary model allocations describe the
# undiscounted counterfactual and never cause an extra debit. Callers supply
# ordinary costs, trigger-visible quotes, and one achievement family.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_equipment_bundle = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_instruments }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_support }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_affordability = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid value = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_paid value = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment value = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_affordability value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_1 = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_2 = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_3 = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_stock = num_equipment@support_equipment_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_1 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_1 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_1 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_2 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_3 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_instruments }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_1 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_2 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_3 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 }
			# Rows without a paid model are not recorded. Transfer their ordinary
			# counterfactual share to a model that the sale actually consumed.
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_1 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining }
			}
			else = { add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_remaining } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_remaining value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 < black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_1 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 < black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 < black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { support_equipment_1 < black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_paid } }
				}
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_1 }
				black_friday_cbrn_bundle_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_2 }
				black_friday_cbrn_bundle_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.cbrn_instrument_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_stock_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_paid_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_3 }
				black_friday_cbrn_bundle_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_stock }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_paid }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_support }
				black_friday_cbrn_bundle_pay_record_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_settle_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
					universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_settle_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
						add_to_variable = { cbrn_diplomacy_instruments_spent = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_family_id }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_instruments }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_paid }
						black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. The inspection owner retains its exact target and ordinary path.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_demand_inspections = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_inspection_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
			has_event_target = cbrn_diplomacy_target_country
			event_target:cbrn_diplomacy_target_country = {
				exists = yes
				cbrn_target_can_receive_inspection_demand_from_root = yes
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_inspection_instrument_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_inspection_support_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_can_pay_cbrn_demand_inspections = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_instruments = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.inspection_instruments }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_support = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.inspection_support }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_cost = black_friday_cbrn_inspection_instrument_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_cost = black_friday_cbrn_inspection_support_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_pay_cbrn_equipment_bundle = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_inspection_payment_success = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success }
	}
}

# The ordinary branch keeps the existing CBRN owner payment and mission path.
black_friday_apply_cbrn_inspection_demand = {
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
		}
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_inspection_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_pay_cbrn_demand_inspections = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_inspection_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
			cbrn_diplomacy_open_paid_inspection_demand = yes
		}
	}
	else = { cbrn_diplomacy_send_inspection_demand = yes }
}

# Country scope with FROM as the exact controlled state. The owner must have
# locked one forensic record before this payer-scoped material purchase runs.
# The native decision modifier owns the one-factory commitment for its timer.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_forensic_publication = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_forensic_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
			FROM = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				has_state_flag = cbrn_forensic_publication_active
				has_variable = cbrn_forensic_selected_source
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_instruments = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.forensic_instruments }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_ordinary_support = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.forensic_support }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_instrument_cost = black_friday_cbrn_forensic_instrument_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_support_cost = black_friday_cbrn_forensic_support_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_pay_cbrn_equipment_bundle = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_forensic_payment_success = black_friday_cbrn_bundle_payment_success }
	}
}

# Country scope with FROM as the exact state. Selection locks the historical
# chemical row or biological seed before payment. A failed sale payment clears
# that lock, allowing the decision's cancel trigger to end the native timer.
# A successful purchase settles at selection; remove_effect publishes later.
black_friday_apply_cbrn_forensic_publication = {
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					cbrn_state_has_shareable_forensic_evidence = yes
				}
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_forensic_instrument_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_forensic_support_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_cbrn_forensic_materials_are_affordable = yes
			}
			FROM = { cbrn_diplomacy_begin_forensic_publication = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { has_state_flag = cbrn_forensic_publication_active } }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_forensic_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_pay_cbrn_forensic_publication = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_forensic_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
					FROM = { cbrn_diplomacy_cancel_forensic_publication = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		FROM = { cbrn_diplomacy_begin_forensic_publication = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_state_flag = cbrn_forensic_publication_active } }
			cbrn_diplomacy_pay_forensic_publication_cost = yes
		}
	}
}
