# Country scope. One planned concrete draw. The caller snapshots stock before
# payment, then checks the exact model balance after the native debit.
black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment_1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_2 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment_2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_3 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment_3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_4 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment_4 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment_1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_2 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment_2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_3 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment_3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment_0 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@motorized_equipment_0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after = num_equipment@motorized_equipment_1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_after }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual value = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = black_friday_cbrn_decon_receipt_source_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_group }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_1 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_2 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_3 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_4 } } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual }
				universal_cost_credit_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_actual }
				universal_cost_credit_component = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. Plans every oldest-first draw before the first debit for the
# requested ordinary or sale variant. Requotes must match the current variant.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_decon_sponsorship = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_variant_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_receipt_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_requested_sale_variant value = constant:universal_cost_framework.two compare = equals }
			black_friday_cbrn_decon_sale_source_is_active = yes
			FROM = { cbrn_state_can_receive_international_decontamination_from_root = yes }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_cbrn_decon_sale_materials_are_affordable = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_masks }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_variant_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_receipt_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_requested_sale_variant value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
			NOT = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_sale_source_is_active = yes }
			FROM = { cbrn_state_can_receive_international_decontamination_from_root = yes }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_materials_are_affordable = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_requote = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_masks }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_requote = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_requote = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_variant_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_variant_valid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_cost compare = equals }

			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_motorized_equipment_0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_motorized_equipment_1 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_motorized_equipment_0 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_motorized_equipment_1 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_motorized_equipment_1 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_motorized_equipment_0 value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_ordinary_remaining }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_all_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_remaining value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_remaining value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_remaining value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
				}
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_gas_mask_equipment_reconditioned }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_gas_mask_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_gas_mask_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_gas_mask_equipment_1 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_gas_mask_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_gas_mask_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_gas_mask_equipment_2 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_gas_mask_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_gas_mask_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_gas_mask_equipment_3 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.gas_mask_equipment_4 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_gas_mask_equipment_4 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_gas_mask_equipment_4 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_gas_mask_equipment_4 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_decontamination_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_decontamination_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_decontamination_equipment_1 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_decontamination_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_decontamination_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_decontamination_equipment_2 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_decontamination_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_decontamination_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_decontamination_equipment_3 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment_0 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_motorized_equipment_0 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_motorized_equipment_0 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_0 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_before = black_friday_cbrn_decon_stock_motorized_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_decon_paid_motorized_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_component_ordinary = black_friday_cbrn_decon_ordinary_motorized_equipment_1 }
				black_friday_cbrn_decon_pay_record_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_settle_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
					universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_settle_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
						add_to_variable = { cbrn_diplomacy_masks_spent = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_requote }
						add_to_variable = { cbrn_diplomacy_decon_equipment_spent = black_friday_cbrn_decon_equipment_requote }
						add_to_variable = { cbrn_diplomacy_trucks_spent = black_friday_cbrn_decon_trucks_requote }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_requested_sale_variant value = constant:universal_cost_framework.two compare = equals } }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.diplomatic }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_diplomacy_cost.decon_masks }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_decon_masks_requote }
							black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
						}
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope, FROM exact target state. Native timed decision already
# reserved one factory; failure leaves no mission flag for cancel_trigger.
black_friday_apply_cbrn_decon_sponsorship = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_requested_sale_variant value = constant:universal_cost_framework.two compare = equals }
					black_friday_cbrn_decon_sale_source_is_active = yes
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_requested_sale_variant value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
					NOT = { black_friday_cbrn_decon_sale_source_is_active = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		black_friday_pay_cbrn_decon_sponsorship = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_decon_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
			FROM = { cbrn_diplomacy_begin_international_decon_mission = yes }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CBRN shelter owner adapter
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY SCOPE, with FROM as the targeted state. The CBRN owner consumes
# support_equipment_1 and train_equipment_1, so this adapter uses the concrete
# native resource kinds rather than the aggregate archetype kinds. All three
# components are quoted and preflighted before the first debit, then retained
# in one payer-scoped receipt for exact refund or settlement.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_move_civilians_to_shelters = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_is_active = yes
			universal_cost_source_is_active = yes
		}
		FROM = { cbrn_calculate_state_raid_response_costs = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_protection_decision_cost.move_to_shelters_pp }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_pp_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_pp_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }

		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = FROM.cbrn_response_shelter_support_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }

		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = FROM.cbrn_response_shelter_train_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.trains }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_train_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.train_equipment_1 }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_train_affordable = universal_cost_quote_affordable }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_pp_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_train_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }

			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_pp_paid }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			universal_cost_pay_component = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_protection_decision_cost.move_to_shelters_pp }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_pp_paid }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_pp_paid }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_paid }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = FROM.cbrn_response_shelter_support_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_paid }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_paid }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
					# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment } }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
					universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					}
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.train_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_train_paid }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_train_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.train_equipment_1 }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = FROM.cbrn_response_shelter_train_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_train_paid }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_train_paid }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
					# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment } }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
					universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					}
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.state_project }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = FROM.cbrn_response_shelter_support_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_support_paid }
				black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

black_friday_apply_cbrn_civilian_shelter_movement = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_shelter_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_pay_cbrn_move_civilians_to_shelters = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_shelter_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
			FROM = { cbrn_complete_civilian_shelter_movement = yes }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { cbrn_move_to_shelters_political_power_debit = constant:cbrn_protection_decision_cost.move_to_shelters_pp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { cbrn_move_to_shelters_political_power_debit = constant:bio_lifecycle_math.negation }
		add_political_power = cbrn_move_to_shelters_political_power_debit
		FROM = { cbrn_apply_civilian_shelter_movement = yes }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Alien infantry landing reservation owner adapter
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope. The voluntary landing decision is the only discounted caller;
# Event 019, sovereignty batches, and direct public-API calls remain fixed at
# the ordinary reserve. The paid amount and receipt id persist for the full
# seven-day refundable reservation.
black_friday_begin_alien_infantry_landing_reservation = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:alien_infantry_landing.reserve_equipment
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum
		}
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { alien_infantry_landing_reserve_amount = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
		alien_infantry_begin_landing_reservation_with_amount = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = alien_infantry_landing_reservation_result
					value = constant:alien_infantry_landing.one
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_alien_laser_reserve
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.custom_adapter
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = constant:alien_infantry_landing.reserve_equipment
			}
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = alien_infantry_landing_reserve_amount }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = alien_infantry_landing_reserve_amount }
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_alien_laser_reserve
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_alien_laser_reserve }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_alien_laser_reserve } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result
						value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				set_temp_variable = {
					black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = constant:alien_infantry_landing.reserve_equipment
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = alien_infantry_landing_reserve_amount }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
			else = {
				alien_infantry_cancel_landing_reservation = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		alien_infantry_begin_landing_reservation = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. The owner has already credited the exact reserved amount.
# Acknowledge that custom component before closing the receipt so the generic
# refund helper cannot issue a second credit.
black_friday_refund_alien_infantry_landing_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id }
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_transaction_id = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_alien_laser_reserve
		}
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_mark_component_refunded_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_mark_component_refunded = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_mark_component_refunded_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id }
			black_friday_retract_achievement_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_refund_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_refund_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
				clear_variable = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Successful materialization settles the refundable receipt.
# Achievement credit is confirmed only if the same natural sale remains active.
black_friday_settle_alien_infantry_landing_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id }
		set_temp_variable = {
			universal_cost_transaction_id = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id
		}
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_settle_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_settle_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id }
			black_friday_confirm_achievement_transaction = yes
			clear_variable = alien_infantry_landing_universal_transaction_id
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Africa elephant logistics owner adapter
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope. The Africa package owns the custom elephant-equipment debit;
# the shared ledger still records it beside the native political-power,
# motorized-equipment, train, and fuel components.
black_friday_record_africa_elephant_logistics_component = {
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_ordinary_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id
				value = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment }
	}
	# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
	# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment } }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
	universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result
				value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success
				compare = not_equals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.custom_adapter compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_refund_amount = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = chaosx_elephant_equipment_1
				amount = black_friday_africa_logistics_refund_amount
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid }
			universal_cost_credit_component = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. The custom equipment row is credited explicitly before the
# shared refund loop, because custom_adapter is deliberately not a native
# payment kind. This helper is used only for an atomic payment failure; the
# successful logistics commitment is non-refundable and is settled after the
# Africa owner effect starts the mission.
black_friday_refund_africa_elephant_logistics_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_lookup_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_lookup_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_lookup_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_found_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
	universal_cost_find_component_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_lookup_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_found_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = {
				var = universal_cost_component_refunded_states^universal_cost_component_found_index
				value = constant:universal_cost_component_state.not_refunded
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_refund_amount = universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^universal_cost_component_found_index }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = chaosx_elephant_equipment_1
			amount = black_friday_africa_logistics_refund_amount
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_mark_component_refunded_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_mark_component_refunded = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_mark_component_refunded_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_refund_amount = constant:universal_cost_framework.negative_one }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 amount = black_friday_africa_logistics_refund_amount }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
	universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope. All five components are quoted and preflighted before any
# debit. A successful active-sale payment leaves the normal owner effect to
# start the contract, while ordinary launches retain the existing owner debit.
black_friday_pay_africa_elephant_logistics_contract = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { black_friday_is_active = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_command_power_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_political_power_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_political_power_paid }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_quote_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }

		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_elephant_equipment_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_elephant_equipment_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 value = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_elephant_equipment_paid compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_truck_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_motorized_equipment_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_motorized_equipment_paid }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_quote_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }

		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_train_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.trains }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_train_equipment_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.train_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_train_equipment_paid }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_quote_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }

		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_fuel_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.fuel }
		universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_fuel_paid = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_fuel_paid }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_quote_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } } set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_all_affordable value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }

			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_political_power_paid }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			universal_cost_pay_component = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_command_power_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_political_power_paid }
				black_friday_record_africa_elephant_logistics_component = yes
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_elephant_debit = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_elephant_equipment_paid }
				multiply_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_elephant_debit = constant:universal_cost_framework.negative_one }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 amount = black_friday_africa_logistics_elephant_debit }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.custom_adapter }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_elephant_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_elephant_equipment_commitment }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_elephant_equipment_paid }
				black_friday_record_africa_elephant_logistics_component = yes
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_motorized_equipment_paid }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_motorized_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.motorized_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_truck_commitment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_motorized_equipment_paid }
					black_friday_record_africa_elephant_logistics_component = yes
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.train_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_train_equipment_paid }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_train_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.train_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_train_commitment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_train_equipment_paid }
					black_friday_record_africa_elephant_logistics_component = yes
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.fuel }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_fuel_paid }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_fuel }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.fuel }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_ordinary_cost = constant:africa_elephant_operation.logistics_fuel_commitment }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_current_paid = black_friday_africa_elephant_logistics_fuel_paid }
					black_friday_record_africa_elephant_logistics_component = yes
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_africa_logistics_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { africa_elephant_logistics_universal_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_variable = { africa_elephant_logistics_materials_already_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			else = {
				black_friday_refund_africa_elephant_logistics_transaction = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

black_friday_activate_sale = {
	set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { black_friday_is_active = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { global.black_friday_sale_sequence = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_activation_date = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_expiration_date = global.num_days }
		add_to_variable = { global.black_friday_expiration_date = constant:black_friday_event.active_duration_days }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_ratio = constant:black_friday_event.baseline_payment_ratio }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_percent = constant:black_friday_event.baseline_discount_percent }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.chaos_meter_value
					value = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_chaos
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:black_friday_event.id }
			set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_type }
			set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_stage_i }
			set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_tier_i }
			if = {
				limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
				set_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_ratio = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_payment_ratio }
				set_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_percent = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_discount_percent }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_force } }
			set_global_flag = black_friday_force_disqualified
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_manual } }
			set_global_flag = black_friday_manual_disqualified
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_natural } }
			set_global_flag = black_friday_natural_activation
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = black_friday_natural_reservation }
			set_global_flag = black_friday_natural_activation
		}
		else = {
			set_global_flag = black_friday_manual_disqualified
		}
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_natural_reservation
		clr_global_flag = black_friday_reserved
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_reservation_date = constant:black_friday_event.zero_date }
		set_global_flag = black_friday_active
		set_global_flag = black_friday_fired
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_state = constant:black_friday_event.state_active }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_ratio = global.black_friday_discount_ratio }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_eligibility_mask = constant:universal_cost_framework.source_all_mask }
		universal_cost_source_register_black_friday = yes
		black_friday_price_table_apply = yes
		black_friday_record_activation_history = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.black_friday_discount_ratio = constant:black_friday_event.evolution_payment_ratio } }
			black_friday_record_evolution_i = yes
		}
		every_country = {
			black_friday_refresh_active_cost_modifier = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no }
				black_friday_prepare_achievement_progress = yes
				black_friday_engine_purchase_snapshot = yes
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr26.2 }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_success }
		log = "CHAOS REDUX BLACK FRIDAY: Sale active at [?global.black_friday_discount_percent]% for the current date"
	}
}

black_friday_reserve_event = {
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_can_reserve = yes }
		set_global_flag = black_friday_reserved
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_state = constant:black_friday_event.state_reserved }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_reservation_date = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_success }
		# Natural provenance must survive after the transient dispatch mode resets.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_natural } }
			set_global_flag = black_friday_natural_reservation
		}
		else = {
			clr_global_flag = black_friday_natural_reservation
		}
		log = "CHAOS REDUX BLACK FRIDAY: Event 26 reserved while waiting for Friday"
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_natural } }
			# The selection transaction resolves its timer before same-day activation
			# can clear the pending reservation.
			calculate_next_timer_value = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { black_friday_is_friday = yes }
			black_friday_activate_sale = yes
		}
	}
}

black_friday_select_or_activate = {
	set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
	black_friday_initialize_runtime = yes
	black_friday_update_calendar_flag = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_event_system_is_running = yes
			NOT = { black_friday_is_disabled = yes }
			check_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_force }
			NOT = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
			NOT = { black_friday_is_fired = yes }
		}
		black_friday_activate_sale = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		log = "CHAOS REDUX BLACK FRIDAY: Duplicate launch ignored while sale is active"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { black_friday_can_activate_naturally = yes }
		black_friday_activate_sale = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_reserved = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { black_friday_can_activate_naturally = yes }
			black_friday_activate_sale = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		black_friday_reserve_event = yes
	}
}

black_friday_dispatch_selected_event = {
	set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_manual }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = temp_bypass_checks
			check_variable = { temp_bypass_checks > 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_force }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = force_trigger_mode_enabled }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_force }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = automatic_event_firing_context
			check_variable = { automatic_event_firing_context > 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_natural }
	}
	set_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr26.1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.black_friday_launch_result > 0 }
			black_friday_is_active = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { event_fire_dispatched = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { event_fire_dispatched = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.launch_no_op }
}

black_friday_daily_pulse = {
	black_friday_initialize_runtime = yes
	black_friday_update_calendar_flag = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = world_end }
		black_friday_terminal_cleanup = yes
	}
	else = {
		black_friday_on_event_disabled = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				black_friday_is_active = yes
				check_variable = {
					var = global.num_days
					value = global.black_friday_expiration_date
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			black_friday_expire_sale = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { black_friday_can_activate_naturally = yes }
			black_friday_activate_sale = yes
		}
	}
}

black_friday_on_chaos_changed = {
	black_friday_initialize_runtime = yes
	black_friday_update_calendar_flag = yes
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_can_activate_naturally = yes }
		black_friday_activate_sale = yes
	}
}

# Resume hook for the edge case where a reservation survives the daily pulse
# while the event system is paused and the same eligible Friday is resumed
# later that day. The normal daily pulse remains the primary path.
black_friday_on_event_system_resumed = {
	black_friday_initialize_runtime = yes
	black_friday_update_calendar_flag = yes
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_can_activate_naturally = yes }
		black_friday_activate_sale = yes
	}
}

black_friday_entry_event = {
	black_friday_initialize_runtime = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.black_friday_dispatch_mode } }
		set_variable = { global.black_friday_dispatch_mode = constant:black_friday_event.dispatch_manual }
	}
	black_friday_select_or_activate = yes
}

# Country scope. Inputs are the ordinary PP and exact support_equipment_1
# amounts plus the trigger-side displayed quotes. This helper does not apply
# the CBRN project outcome or debit the owner's legacy payment effects.
# Output: black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_payment_success.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_protection_pair = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_sale = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
