	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_quote_matches = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_pp }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_support }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_sale = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
		if = {
			limit = { universal_cost_source_is_active = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_pp }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_support }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
			universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_cost compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_cost compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_sale value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_source_id value = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_quote_matches = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_quote_matches value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_affordability = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote }
		universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_affordability = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_affordability value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_affordability value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_recorded = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_recorded = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			universal_cost_pay_component = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_pp }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_source_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_recorded = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_requote }
					universal_cost_credit_component = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_recorded value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote }
				universal_cost_pay_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_support }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_source_id }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
					# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_support_equipment } }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
					universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_recorded = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.support_equipment_1 }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote }
						universal_cost_credit_component = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_recorded value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_settle_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_settle_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_sale value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_ordinary_support }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_support_requote }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
						black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_protection_pair_pp_recorded value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. One native component debit and receipt append. The caller
# supplies exact kind/id, ordinary and payable amount, and primary component.
# A paid component whose receipt append fails is credited immediately; earlier
# recorded components are refunded by the enclosing theater transaction.
black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_record_component = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid }
		# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_ordinary }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = black_friday_cbrn_theater_source_id }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_primary }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_group }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_1 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_2 } check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_3 } } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			}
			universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = not_equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid }
				universal_cost_credit_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
	}
}

# Assign the full ordinary decontamination counterfactual to the first model
# actually paid. Other model rows keep zero ordinary cost; their positive paid
# amounts still preserve exact-model stock and refund identity.
black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_decon_model = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_ordinary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_primary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_primary_model_assigned value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_ordinary = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.decontamination_equipment }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_primary = black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id }
		}
		black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_record_component = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_primary_model_assigned = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. The decision owner checks the exact state and applies its
# non-payment outcome only when payment_success is one. Gas masks, support,
# and motorized stock remain nonconsumed requirements.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_theater_assignment = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_assignment_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_can_pay_cbrn_theater_assignment = yes
			cbrn_country_has_theater_decontamination_support_stores = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_sale = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_requote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_requote = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_requote = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.decontamination_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_requote = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.fuel }
		if = {
			limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_sale = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_requote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_source_query_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
			if = {
				limit = { universal_cost_source_is_active = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.command_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.command_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_source_count = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.decontamination_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
				universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.fuel }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_family_mask = constant:universal_cost_family.fuel }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_rounding_quantum = constant:universal_cost_framework.default_rounding_quantum }
				universal_cost_quote_integer = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_requote = universal_cost_quote_final_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_source_count = universal_cost_quote_source_count }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_source_count value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_requote_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_requote_valid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_requote value = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_requote value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_requote }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_stock_1 = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_1 = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_1 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_stock_1 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_stock_2 = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment_2 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_2 = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_2 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_stock_2 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_2 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_stock_3 = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment_3 }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_3 = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_3 min = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero max = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_stock_3 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_3 }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_requote }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_affordable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_preflight = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.fuel }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_quote_final_cost = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_requote }
			universal_cost_check_quote_affordable = yes
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_preflight = universal_cost_quote_affordable }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_remaining value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_preflight value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_preflight value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { decontamination_equipment_1 < black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_1 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { decontamination_equipment_2 < black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_2 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { decontamination_equipment_3 < black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_3 } }
				}
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_primary_model_assigned = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_command_power }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.command_power }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_ordinary = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.command_power }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_theater_command_power_requote }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_primary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_record_component = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_1 }
				black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_decon_model = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_2 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_2 }
				black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_decon_model = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.decontamination_equipment_3 }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decon_paid_3 }
				black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_decon_model = yes
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_fuel }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.fuel }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_ordinary = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.fuel }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_paid = black_friday_cbrn_theater_fuel_requote }
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_component_primary = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_cbrn_theater_pay_record_component = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_all_paid value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_primary_model_assigned value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_settle_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
					universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_settle_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_theater_assignment_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_sale value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.equipment }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_doctrine_cleanup_assignment_cost.decontamination_equipment }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_theater_decontamination_requote }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
							black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_theater_assignment_payment_success value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
					universal_cost_refund_transaction = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. The mask shipment remains in the owner's idempotent state
# escrow; only the PP service fee is a Black Friday purchase component.
black_friday_pay_cbrn_external_aid_pp_fee = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { black_friday_is_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_cost = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				black_friday_can_pay_cbrn_external_aid_pp_fee = yes
				has_event_target = cbrn_external_aid_transaction_target_state
				event_target:cbrn_external_aid_transaction_target_state = {
					has_state_flag = cbrn_external_mask_escrow_active
					cbrn_occupation_state_has_donor_external_aid_reservation = yes
				}
			}
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_owner_transaction_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
			black_friday_allocate_owner_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_cost }
			# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
			set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
			universal_cost_pay_component = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_payment_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_resource_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_occupation_decision_cost.protective_aid_pp }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_quoted_cost = black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_actual_paid_cost = black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_source_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_primary_component = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_record_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				# Registry-owned logical achievement role, independent of paid resource kind.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_cost_basis = constant:universal_cost_achievement_basis.primary_component }
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_component_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power } }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_achievement_primary_member = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				universal_cost_record_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_record_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					event_target:cbrn_external_aid_transaction_target_state = {
						set_variable = { cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_id = black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
						set_variable = { cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_sale_sequence = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power }
					set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_cost }
					universal_cost_credit_component = yes
					clear_variable = universal_cost_allocated_transaction_id_@var:black_friday_owner_transaction_id
				}
			}
			else = { clear_variable = universal_cost_allocated_transaction_id_@var:black_friday_owner_transaction_id }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { cbrn_protection_pp_debit_requested = constant:cbrn_occupation_decision_cost.protective_aid_pp }
		cbrn_debit_protection_political_power = yes
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_pp_payment_success = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
	}
}

# State scope. The payer-scoped receipt closes after the selected target's
# native mask cleanup. Only a successful shipment completion can credit its PP
# family; completion after expiry is tied back to the sale sequence at payment.
black_friday_settle_cbrn_external_aid_pp_fee_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_id }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_id }
		set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_receipt_settled = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_transaction_id value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_transaction_id = universal_cost_transaction_id }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_ordinary_cost = constant:cbrn_occupation_decision_cost.protective_aid_pp }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_sale_sequence = cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_sale_sequence }
			ROOT = {
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_transaction_id = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id }
				# Caller-owned cost API outputs.
				set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_settle_transaction_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
				universal_cost_settle_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = universal_cost_settle_transaction_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_receipt_settled = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_successful_completion value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_lookup_transaction_id = black_friday_purchase_transaction_id }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_lookup_id = constant:black_friday_event.owner_component_political_power }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_found_index = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index }
						set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_component_lookup_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_no_op }
						universal_cost_find_component_row = yes
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_lookup_result value = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success compare = equals }
								check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_found_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.invalid_index compare = greater_than }
								check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_resource_kinds^universal_cost_component_found_index value = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power compare = equals }
								check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_source_ids^universal_cost_component_found_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.black_friday_source_id compare = equals }
								check_variable = { var = universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^universal_cost_component_found_index value = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero compare = greater_than }
							}
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_actual_paid_cost = universal_cost_component_actual_paid_costs^universal_cost_component_found_index }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_political_power_only = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_post_expiry_confirmation = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_is_refundable = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
							black_friday_record_achievement_transaction = yes
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_political_power_only = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
							set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_allow_post_expiry_confirmation = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = black_friday_cbrn_external_aid_receipt_settled value = constant:universal_cost_framework.one compare = equals } }
				clear_variable = cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_id
				clear_variable = cbrn_external_mask_escrow_transaction_sale_sequence
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Simple custom-cost sale API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# COUNTRY scope (payer). Inputs: temps black_friday_simple_cost_ordinary and
# black_friday_simple_cost_kind, as for black_friday_simple_cost_quote. Pays the
# current quote once through universal_cost_pay_component. Output: temp
# black_friday_simple_cost_paid, 1 when the payment went through and 0 when the
# quote was invalid, unaffordable, or in a resource without a native payment.
# A payment priced by a valid natural sale credits its resource family toward
# the achievement; a payment in any resource other than political power also
# counts as the non-political payment. For a multi-resource action, set temp
# black_friday_simple_cost_no_credit = 1 before each secondary component and pay
# the primary component last, so the action credits one family. The flag is
# consumed by each call. No receipt or refund path is kept.
# Contract: common/scripted_effects/026_black_friday_simple_cost_api.md.
black_friday_simple_cost_pay = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_simple_cost_quote = yes
			check_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_valid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_resource_kind = black_friday_simple_cost_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { universal_cost_payment_amount = black_friday_simple_cost_payable }
		universal_cost_pay_component = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { universal_cost_payment_result = constant:universal_cost_framework.result_success } }
			set_temp_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_paid = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_quoted = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
					check_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_payable > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = black_friday_simple_cost_family }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_no_credit = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.one }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_kind = constant:universal_cost_resource_kind.political_power } }
					set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
				}
				black_friday_credit_sale_purchase = yes
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_simple_cost_no_credit = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
}

# COUNTRY scope (payer). Credits one purchase that its owner paid at a sale
# price, from the price table or a simple-API quote, toward the achievement.
# Inputs: temps black_friday_purchase_family_id (constant:universal_cost_family.*,
# zero for no family) and black_friday_purchase_non_political (1 when the
# purchase paid any resource other than political power). Credits only during a
# valid natural sale whose achievement progress is prepared; forced, manual,
# debug, AI, and already-awarded progress stay excluded. Consumes the
# non-political input. Owners call it once per purchase, after paying.
black_friday_credit_sale_purchase = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			black_friday_achievement_is_valid_sale = yes
			has_variable = black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence
			check_variable = { black_friday_achievement_indexed_sale_sequence = global.black_friday_sale_sequence }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.one } }
			add_to_variable = { black_friday_achievement_non_political_receipt_count = constant:black_friday_event.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id > constant:universal_cost_framework.zero } }
			black_friday_add_committed_achievement_family = yes
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
}

# COUNTRY scope (payer). Credits one purchase paid in political power from the
# sale price table toward the Political Power achievement family: sets the
# family and a zero non-political input, then calls
# black_friday_credit_sale_purchase, so it does nothing outside a valid natural
# sale and AI, forced, manual, and debug sales stay excluded. Called once from a
# hidden_effect in the complete_effect of a decision whose native cost reads
# global.black_friday_price_*, and in an event option that pays a political
# power table price.
black_friday_credit_political_power_purchase = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_non_political = constant:universal_cost_framework.zero }
	black_friday_credit_sale_purchase = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope (payer). Withdraws one Political Power credit that
# black_friday_credit_political_power_purchase gave during the current sale,
# for a decision that refunds its charged table price when cancelled. Does
# nothing outside that sale or when no Political Power purchase is committed.
black_friday_withdraw_political_power_purchase = {
	set_temp_variable = { black_friday_purchase_family_id = constant:universal_cost_family.political_power }
	black_friday_remove_committed_achievement_family = yes
}
